- **kilo-cli**: Launch, automate and hand off work to separate Kilo Code CLI instances and their subagents. - **kilo-loop**: Hand a whole task to the free Kilo CLI: Kilo does the work, then audits and fixes its own changes in a loop until two audits in a row find nothing, and only then do you audit the final diff. Run when a task is well defined and you want a cheap model to do the edits while spending as few Claude tokens as possible. Not for investigation where you make the edits — use /kilo; not for small tasks where writing the brief costs as much as doing the work — just do those. ([robertsolves/agent-commands](https://github.com/robertsolves/agent-commands/blob/HEAD/commands/kilo-loop.md))
- **kilo-refine-findings**: Refine a findings document (a bug list, an audit of API bugs, a TODO file, a list of stale comments) with the free Kilo CLI: each round Kilo re-checks every entry against the code, deletes the fixed or false ones, corrects the drifted ones, then adds new entries of the same kind, until two rounds in a row leave the document unchanged; then you spot-check the changes once. Run when a findings document needs a deep refresh while spending as few Claude tokens as possible. Not for one careful pass by you — use /refine-findings; not for doing a task — use /kilo-loop. ([robertsolves/agent-commands](https://github.com/robertsolves/agent-commands/blob/HEAD/commands/kilo-refine-findings.md))

- **kztek-brand-info**: PHẢI load khi: tạo hoặc sửa tài liệu/slide/báo cáo có logo KZTEK, áp dụng màu Navy #251C53 hoặc Cam #F05922, thiết kế slide/Word/Excel theo brand KZTEK, kiểm tra quy tắc đặt logo, chọn màu nền/heading/accent, hoặc bất kỳ output nào cần nhận diện thương hiệu KZTEK. Dùng cả khi agent chưa chắc màu nào là đúng — tốt hơn là load nhầm còn hơn bỏ sót brand. KHÔNG dùng khi: chỉ cần thông tin liên lạc/địa chỉ công ty (tra cứu trực tiếp) hoặc đang làm brand cho tổ chức khác. ([flick-git-anhnv/RemoteControl](https://github.com/flick-git-anhnv/RemoteControl/blob/HEAD/.gemini/commands/kztek-brand-info.md) · ★2 · 6 copies)
